				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}


		VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_volunteers_pending STP_ps_refund_val_supply = yes }
		STS = { STP_cw_remove_kefreyt_volunteers = yes }
		STP = { clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_evidence clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_supply clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_aid_open }
		STS = { clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_evidence clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_strategy_supply clr_country_flag = STP_cw_northern_aid_open }
	}


}

# Close either the expedition or its pending mobilisation before STS disappears.
# Approval alone is not participation: only a deployed NOD receives a victory reward.
STP_cw_close_nod_after_party_victory = {
	NOD = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered has_country_flag = NOD_cw_intervention_approved } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered }
				set_country_flag = NOD_cw_victorious
				add_war_support = 0.05
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.43 }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = STS } white_peace = STS }
			clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered
			clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_intervention_approved
			ADISCORD_economy_update_postwar_demobilization = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_intervention_ready
			remove_mission = NOD_cw_intervention_preparation
			STS = {
				remove_mission = STP_cw_nod_redeployment
				clr_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_warning_active
				clr_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_entered
				remove_mission = STP_cw_nod_warning
			}
			log = "[STP_CW] Party victory closed NOD expedition or pending mobilisation"
			remove_mission = NOD_cw_northern_redeployment
			STP = { remove_mission = STP_cw_nod_warning remove_mission = STP_cw_nod_redeployment clr_country_flag = STP_cw_nod_warning_active }
		}
	}
}

# Winner country scope, original on_capitulation ROOT remains the defeated rival.
STP_cw_settle_union_victory = {
	if = { limit = { tag = STS ROOT = { tag = STP } } ROOT = { STP_ps_prepare_exile = yes } }
	STS = { STP_cw_remove_kefreyt_volunteers = yes }
	log = "[STP_CW] capital occupation settlement: [THIS.GetTag] defeated [ROOT.GetTag]; reported FROM=[FROM.GetTag]"
	if = { limit = { ROOT = { tag = STP } } STP_cw_end_nod_intervention = yes }
	if = { limit = { ROOT = { tag = STS } } STP_cw_close_nod_after_party_victory = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_war_with = ROOT } white_peace = ROOT }
	ROOT = { STP_pf_clear = yes remove_ideas = { STP_cw_party_battle_spirit STP_cw_resistance_battle_spirit } }
	annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = no }
	# The resistance is a claimant to Stelander; foreign conquests stay non-core.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = STP NOT = { is_core_of = STS } }
			add_core_of = STS
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_election_mandate_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_cw_election_mandate_dynamic } }
	set_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle
	if = { limit = { tag = STS } STP_cw_close_last_banquet_technical = yes }
	STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes
	if = { limit = { tag = STS } STP_pw_rebuild_economy_after_unification = yes }
	if = { limit = { tag = STP } news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.71 hours = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = STS } news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.72 hours = 1 } }
	STP_cw_check_union_wars_finished = yes
}

STP_cw_settle_nod_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP = { exists = yes } STS = { exists = yes } STS = { has_capitulated = yes } NOD = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered } }
		STP_cw_close_nod_after_party_victory = yes
		STS = { STP_cw_remove_kefreyt_volunteers = yes }
		STP = {
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = STS } white_peace = STS }
			STS = { remove_ideas = STP_cw_resistance_battle_spirit }
			annex_country = { target = STS transfer_troops = no }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle
			STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.71 hours = 1 }
		}
		log = "[STP_CW] NOD military victory restores STS territory to STP"
		STP_cw_check_union_wars_finished = yes
	}
}


# Only dismantle the alliance created from factionless northern members.
# Leadership may have passed from YPR to another member during the campaign.
STP_cw_dismantle_northern_alliance = {
	YPR = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				has_faction_template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				NOT = { any_other_country = { is_in_faction_with = YPR NOT = { OR = { tag = YPR tag = COF tag = TFF } } } }
			}
			faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
		}
	}
}

# NOD scope. Resolve only the northern campaign; every other war remains intact.
STP_cw_settle_northern_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { STP_cw_northern_campaign_won = yes STP_cw_northern_conference_won = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_northern_conference_won = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 2 }
			# These states have already been awarded to NOD's side by the conference.
			YPR = { transfer_state = 8 transfer_state = 15 transfer_state = 16 transfer_state = 19 transfer_state = 20 transfer_state = 21 transfer_state = 22 }
			TFF = { transfer_state = 83 transfer_state = 84 transfer_state = 85 transfer_state = 86 transfer_state = 87 transfer_state = 303 }
			transfer_state = 14
			if = { limit = { TFF = { is_puppet_of = NOD } } set_autonomy = { target = TFF autonomy_state = autonomy_free end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
			if = { limit = { TFF = { is_in_faction_with = NOD } } remove_from_faction = TFF }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 2 } }
		activate_mission = NOD_cw_northern_redeployment
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = YPR } white_peace = YPR }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = COF } white_peace = COF }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
		clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_war_started
		STP_cw_end_northern_colonial_war = yes
		STP_cw_dismantle_northern_alliance = yes
		YPR = { set_major = no }
		COF = { set_major = no }
		TFF = { set_major = no }
		if = {
			limit = { YPR = { exists = yes is_subject = no STP_cw_ypr_has_only_historical_states = yes } }
			puppet = { target = YPR end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { COF = { exists = yes is_subject = no } }
			annex_country = { target = COF transfer_troops = no }
		}
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.80 }
		STS = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } STP_cw_poll_nod_intervention = yes } }
		log = "[STP_NORTH] NOD victory after three accepted military defeats: YPR historical bounds checked, TFF independent"
	}
}

# Called only by the guarded immediate NOD capitulation callback, before native cap state flips.
STP_cw_settle_northern_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = equals }
			OR = {
				NOD_cw_can_accept_northern_defeat = yes
				AND = { has_war_with = YPR check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 6 compare = equals } }
				AND = { has_war_with = COF check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 7 compare = equals } }
				AND = { has_war_with = TFF check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 8 compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 3 }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = YPR } white_peace = YPR }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = COF } white_peace = COF }
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
		clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_war_started
		STP_cw_end_northern_colonial_war = yes
		YPR = { set_major = no }
		COF = { set_major = no }
		TFF = { set_major = no }
		if = {
			limit = { YPR = { exists = yes is_subject = no } }
			if = { limit = { 17 = { is_owned_by = NOD } } YPR = { transfer_state = 17 } }
			if = { limit = { 18 = { is_owned_by = NOD } } YPR = { transfer_state = 18 } }
		}
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.81 }
		STS = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } STP_cw_poll_nod_intervention = yes } }
		log = "[STP_NORTH] NOD defeated by the north: separate peace, owned 17/18 ceded to YPR"
	}
}

STP_cw_poll_northern_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { STP_cw_northern_campaign_won = yes STP_cw_northern_conference_won = yes } } STP_cw_settle_northern_victory = yes }
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						has_war_with = YPR
						has_war_with = COF
						has_war_with = TFF
					}
				}
			}
			# A foreign peace or annexation cannot count as the coalition's military defeat.
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 4 }
			activate_mission = NOD_cw_northern_redeployment
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = YPR } white_peace = YPR }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = COF } white_peace = COF }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
			clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_northern_war_started
			STP_cw_end_northern_colonial_war = yes
			YPR = { set_major = no }
			COF = { set_major = no }
			TFF = { set_major = no }
			log = "[STP_NORTH] Northern campaign closed after external peace; no scripted territorial award"
		}
	}
}

# AIN can fight only while it remains NOD's colony; an independent war is its own.
STP_cw_end_northern_colonial_war = {
	AIN = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_puppet_of = NOD }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = YPR } white_peace = YPR }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = COF } white_peace = COF }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: keep accumulated values when a later focus refreshes the institution.
STP_pw_begin_recovery = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS } NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_recovery_progress } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_progress value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_services value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_industry value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_army value = 0 }
		STP_pw_update_recovery = yes
	}
}

# STS: rebase the economy after the civil-war winner annexes the old union.
# STS was initialized while it owned only the resistance rump, so its cached
# factory/resource sources must be rebuilt after unification or the dashboard
# can keep showing the wartime fragment's near-zero economy.
STP_pw_rebuild_economy_after_unification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_economy_important_country
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_full_refresh = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_light_update = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 50 compare = less_than } }
			ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_update_postwar_demobilization = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_update_gui = yes
	}
}

# Three funded programs remove the reconstruction burden in equal steps.
# STS: save-safe initialization for active postwar gameplay.
# The three values are deliberately country variables so the decision category
# can expose them directly without a bespoke GUI.
STP_pw_reconcile_postwar_interactivity = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_reconstruction_momentum } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_reconstruction_momentum value = 25 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_public_confidence } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_public_confidence value = 35 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_regional_cohesion } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_regional_cohesion value = 30 } }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pw_reconstruction_momentum min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pw_public_confidence min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_pw_regional_cohesion min = 0 max = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_pc_development_country_must_live
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_propaganda_slot_1 }
			}
			ADISCORD_campaign_slot_grant = yes
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_propaganda_slot_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = STP_pc_development_generation_reconstruction
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_propaganda_slot_2 }
			}
			ADISCORD_campaign_slot_grant = yes
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_propaganda_slot_2
		}
	}
}

STP_pw_update_recovery = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_burden value = 3 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_burden value = STP_pw_recovery_progress }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_burden min = 0 max = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_output value = STP_pw_recovery_burden }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_output value = -0.04 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_construction value = STP_pw_recovery_burden }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_construction value = -0.05 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_expense value = STP_pw_recovery_burden }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_expense value = 0.04 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_burden value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_reconstruction_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_reconstruction_dynamic } }
	}
	else_if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_reconstruction_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_reconstruction_dynamic } }
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# The deposit is the shared slot and the receipt; clear it before returning money.
STP_pw_cancel_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_pw_project_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_refund value = STP_pw_project_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_deposit
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_kind
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_pw_project_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_pw_project_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pw_finish_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = STP_pw_project_deposit
			STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
			capital_scope = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 1 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 720 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 250 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 80 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 96 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_services value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 2 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 1080 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 120 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 144 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_industry value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 3 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 540 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 100 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 60 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 72 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_army value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_services value = 1 }
			add_stability = 0.05
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = -0.05 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_industry value = 1 }
			add_offsite_building = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_army value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_army_org_regain value = 0.10 }
			army_experience = 25
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_progress value = 1 }
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_deposit
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_kind
		STP_pw_update_recovery = yes
		STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	}
	else = { STP_pw_cancel_project = yes }
}

STP_pw_refresh_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_overall_income_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_overall_income_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_treasury_capacity_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_treasury_capacity_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_creditworthiness_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_creditworthiness_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_global_growth_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_global_growth_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_economic_growth_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_economic_growth_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_state_growth_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_state_growth_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_society_growth_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_country_development_society_growth_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_army_org_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_army_org_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_army_org_regain } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_army_org_regain value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_industrial_capacity_factory } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_planning_speed } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_planning_speed value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_political_power_gain } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_political_power_gain value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_production_speed_buildings_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_production_speed_buildings_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_production_speed_industrial_complex_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_research_speed_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_research_speed_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_stability_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_stability_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_supply_consumption_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_pw_training_time_factor } } set_variable = { var = STP_pw_training_time_factor value = 0 } }
		if = { limit = { tag = STS NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_republic_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_republic_dynamic } }
		if = { limit = { tag = STP NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_party_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pw_party_dynamic } }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}


# COUNTRY: 70 / 90 warnings and guaranteed arrest before the election closes.
STP_pc_update_suspicion_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_shabrat_was_arrested }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 90 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_90 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_90
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_70 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
		}
		set_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_70
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.1 }
	}
}

# STS: bookkeeping that change_tag_from does not copy.
STP_pc_copy_split_flags = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_archives_used } } set_country_flag = STP_cw_archives_used }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_shabrat_was_arrested } } set_country_flag = STP_shabrat_was_arrested }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_gornin_introduced } } set_country_flag = STP_gornin_introduced }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_vera_introduced } } set_country_flag = STP_vera_introduced }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_kefreyt_shipment_received } } set_country_flag = STP_cw_kefreyt_shipment_received }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_70 } } set_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_70 }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_90 } } set_country_flag = STP_pc_warned_90 }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag } } set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag }
		if = { limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag } } set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = STP.STP_pc_course }
		set_variable = { var = STP_party_suspicion value = STP.STP_party_suspicion }
	}
}

# STS: the displayed head of state is the ruling party's leader. Promote first,
# then switch the party to that registered subtype so a generated stand-in cannot remain.
STP_pc_align_resistance_ruling_party = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_shabrat_available = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ideology = national_legitimism }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = chauvinism elections_allowed = no }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
				promote_character = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ideology = national_legitimism }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_sotnikov_available = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ideology = steland_military_directory }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
				promote_character = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ideology = steland_military_directory }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character = STP_Leonid_Barchel STP_Leonid_Barchel = { NOT = { has_character_flag = STP_cw_arrested } } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
			}
			promote_character = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ideology = chauvinism_ideology }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = chauvinism elections_allowed = no }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ruling_only = yes } } }
				retire_country_leader = yes
				promote_character = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ideology = chauvinism_ideology }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: arrest can land on STP or STS; the successor is always installed on STS.
STP_pc_install_resistance_successor = {
	STS = { mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { STS = { exists = yes } }
		STS = {
			STP_cw_establish_resistance_command = yes
			STP_pc_align_resistance_ruling_party = yes
		}
	}
}

# STS: lock course 1. Freedom is closed.
STP_pc_lock_hegemony = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 1 }
	set_country_flag = STP_pc_course_locked
	STP_maksim_shabrat = {
		add_country_leader_role = {
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = { ideology = chauvinism_ideology expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
		}
	}
	set_politics = { ruling_party = chauvinism elections_allowed = no }
}

# STS: lock course 2. Hegemony is closed. Vanilla elections stay off.
STP_pc_lock_freedom = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 2 }
	set_country_flag = STP_pc_course_locked
	STP_maksim_shabrat = {
		add_country_leader_role = {
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = { ideology = steland_liberationism expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
		}
	}
	set_politics = { ruling_party = humanism elections_allowed = no }
	add_popularity = { ideology = humanism popularity = 0.15 }
}

# STS: lock course 3 for Sotnikov or Barchel.
STP_pc_lock_directory = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 3 }
	set_country_flag = STP_pc_course_locked
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_sotnikov_available = yes }
		STP_grigory_sotnikov = {
			add_country_leader_role = {
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = steland_military_directory expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
			}
		}
		promote_character = { character = STP_grigory_sotnikov ideology = steland_military_directory }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_character = STP_Leonid_Barchel STP_Leonid_Barchel = { NOT = { has_character_flag = STP_cw_arrested } } }
		STP_Leonid_Barchel = {
			add_country_leader_role = {
				promote_leader = yes
				country_leader = { ideology = steland_military_directory expire = "2200.1.1.1" }
			}
		}
		promote_character = { character = STP_Leonid_Barchel ideology = steland_military_directory }
	}
	set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
}

# STS: scripted war. Diplomatic UI stays closed; the native war relation is
# the single source of truth for the later capitulation settlement.
STP_pc_declare_war_val = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS STP_pc_can_confront_val = yes NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
		declare_war_on = { target = VAL type = annex_everything }
	}
}

STP_pc_declare_war_nod = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS STP_pc_can_confront_nod = yes NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } }
		declare_war_on = { target = NOD type = annex_everything }
	}
}

STP_pc_declare_liberation_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_val_crisis
			VAL = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
		}
		STP_pc_declare_war_val = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_crisis
			NOD = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NOD }
		}
		STP_pc_declare_war_nod = yes
	}
}

STP_pc_form_hegemony_league = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_hegemony_league } }
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_hegemony_league
			create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = STP_pc_hegemony_league }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VAL = {
					exists = yes
					OR = { has_idea = STP_pc_val_client is_subject_of = STS }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS }
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = VAL
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOD = {
					exists = yes
					OR = { has_idea = STP_pc_nod_client is_subject_of = STS }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS }
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = NOD
		}
	}
}

STP_pc_form_liberation_coalition = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_liberation_coalition } }
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_liberation_coalition
			create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = STP_pc_liberation_coalition }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				VAL = {
					exists = yes
					has_idea = VAL_liberated_settlement
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS }
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = VAL
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOD = {
					exists = yes
					has_idea = NOD_mandate_broken
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS }
				}
			}
			add_to_faction = NOD
		}
	}
}

STP_pc_offer_liberation_package = {
	# A country removed before answering cannot keep the next recipient waiting.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_lib_package_tag value = 1 compare = equals } VAL = { STP_pc_liberation_recipient_available = yes } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_lib_package_tag value = 2 compare = equals } NOD = { STP_pc_liberation_recipient_available = yes } }
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending
		clear_variable = STP_pc_lib_package_tag
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_val_won
			VAL = {
				STP_pc_liberation_recipient_available = yes
				NOT = { has_idea = VAL_liberated_settlement }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_val_refused_package }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pc_lib_package_tag value = 1 }
		set_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending
		VAL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.11 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_nod_won
			NOD = {
				STP_pc_liberation_recipient_available = yes
				NOT = { has_idea = NOD_mandate_broken }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_refused_package }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_pc_lib_package_tag value = 2 }
		set_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending
		NOD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.11 } }
	}
	else = {
		clear_variable = STP_pc_lib_package_tag
	}
}

# STS: return territorial receipts from Kefreyt before the technical peace.
# All current STS/STP cores held by VAL or a VAL subject return automatically.
# 52 and 55 are explicit contractual exceptions and become STS cores on recovery.
STP_pc_recover_stelander_cores_from_val = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = STS
					is_core_of = STP
					state = 42
					state = 52
					state = 55
				}
				OR = {
					is_owned_by = VAL
					owner = { is_subject_of = VAL }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_core_of = STS } }
				add_core_of = STS
			}
			transfer_state_to = STS
			set_state_controller_to = STS
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

# STS: freeze the occupier snapshot and close only that pair.
# Liberation victories are recorded here; later focuses keep optional neighbor terms.
STP_pc_begin_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_cap_side value = 1 compare = equals } }
			# Reaching this branch already means VAL capitulated in the reserved STS war.
			# Do not re-derive the result from a controller snapshot that may have flipped.
			set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 1 }
			STP_pc_recover_stelander_cores_from_val = yes
			VAL = { VAL_enter_stelander_defeat = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_cap_side value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 2 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 1 }
			NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_pc_defeated_by_sts }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD } white_peace = NOD }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_cap_side value = 3 compare = equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 5 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 1 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 2 }
				if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 4 compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 2 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 2 }
				if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD } white_peace = NOD }
			}
		}
		VAL_enforce_stelander_defeat = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pc_cap_side value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_val_won
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = STP_pc_cap_side value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_nod_won
		}
		clear_variable = STP_pc_this_opponent
		clear_variable = STP_pc_this_result
		clear_variable = STP_pc_cap_side
		STP_pc_clear_settlement = yes
	}
}

STP_pc_clear_settlement = {
	clear_variable = STP_pc_settle_opponent
	clear_variable = STP_pc_settle_result
	clear_variable = STP_pc_queued_opponent
	clear_variable = STP_pc_queued_result
	clr_country_flag = STP_pc_settlement_pending
	# The second neighbor can fall after both package focuses have finished.
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = STP_pc_lib_transition }
		STP_pc_offer_liberation_package = yes
	}
}

# Recipient country: authenticate before touching the shared STS receipt.
STP_pc_finish_liberation_package = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pc_liberation_reply_matches_receipt = yes }
		STS = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_package_pending
			clear_variable = STP_pc_lib_package_tag
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pc_liberation_coalition }
				STP_pc_form_liberation_coalition = yes
			}
			STP_pc_offer_liberation_package = yes
		}
	}
}

# Imperial union

# Mechanical formation of the Stelander Imperial Union. The matching news event
# is presentation-only; it must never annex or transfer territory on its own.

STP_proclaim_imperial_union = {
	set_country_flag = STP_imperial_union_proclaimed
	set_cosmetic_tag = STP_empire

	set_politics = {
		ruling_party = chauvinism
		elections_allowed = no
		long_name = STP_empire_faction_party_long
		name = STP_empire_faction_party
	}

	add_country_leader_role = {
		character = STP_maksim_shabrat
		promote_leader = yes
		country_leader = {
			ideology = chauvinism_ideology
			expire = "2190.1.1.1"
			traits = { }
		}
	}
	set_country_leader_portrait = {
		ideology = chauvinism
		portrait = GFX_portrait_STP_Maksim_Shabrat_dictator
	}

	news_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent_news.2 }
}

# STS: one civil-war operation cycle. The pending receipt separates mission
# settlement from the next activation in the native decision update.
STP_cw_begin_operations = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_war_with = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_cw_participant
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_cw_operation_round }
		}
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_pending value = 1 }
		STP = { add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_party_command_disruption days = 35 } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.92 hours = 1 }
	}
}

STP_cw_dispatch_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS has_variable = STP_cw_operation_pending NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_cw_front_operation } }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_pending
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = STP has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar } check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 3 compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { 2 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 2 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 29 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 29 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 53 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 53 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 3 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 3 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 46 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 46 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 45 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 45 } }
			else_if = { limit = { 28 = { is_controlled_by = STP } } set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 28 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 1 }
				activate_mission = STP_cw_front_operation
			}
			else = { set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 3 } clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_target }
		}
		else = { STP_cw_close_operation_cycle = yes }
	}
}

STP_cw_win_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_war_with = STP
			has_capitulated = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_cw_operation_pending }
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 2 compare = equals } 2 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 29 compare = equals } 29 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 53 compare = equals } 53 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 3 compare = equals } 3 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 46 compare = equals } 46 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 45 compare = equals } 45 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 28 compare = equals } 28 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
			}
		}
		# Consume the objective before rewards so replayed callbacks cannot pay again.
		clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_target
		remove_ideas = STP_cw_operation_disorganization
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_operation_tempo days = 21 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 3 compare = less_than } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_pending value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.92 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_fail_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STS
			has_war_with = STP
			has_capitulated = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar }
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_cw_operation_pending }
			check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		# Control can change on the expiry hour; a completed objective takes priority.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 2 compare = equals } 2 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 29 compare = equals } 29 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 53 compare = equals } 53 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 3 compare = equals } 3 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 46 compare = equals } 46 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 45 compare = equals } 45 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_target value = 28 compare = equals } 28 = { is_controlled_by = STS } }
				}
			}
			STP_cw_win_operation = yes
		}
		else = {
			clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_target
			remove_ideas = STP_cw_operation_tempo
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_cw_operation_disorganization days = 28 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 3 compare = less_than } }
				set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_pending value = 1 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.92 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Safe inside cancel_effect: never remove the mission executing this callback.
STP_cw_close_operation_cycle = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_cw_operation_round value = 3 }
	clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_target
	clear_variable = STP_cw_operation_pending
	remove_ideas = { STP_cw_operation_tempo STP_cw_operation_disorganization }
	STP = { remove_ideas = STP_cw_party_command_disruption }
}

# Settlement hook: close the receipt before removing a still-active mission.
STP_cw_stop_operations = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = STS has_variable = STP_cw_operation_round }
		STP_cw_close_operation_cycle = yes
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = STP_cw_front_operation } remove_mission = STP_cw_front_operation }
	}
}

# Recipient accepts before either treasury changes; clear the receipt before delivery.
STP_pc_settle_val_compact = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pc_val_offer_current = yes STS = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_val_offer_kind value = 1 compare = equals } STP_pc_can_pay_val_compact = yes } }
		STS = {
			clear_variable = STP_pc_val_offer_kind
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_val_terms_accepted
			STP_pc_credit_val_compact = yes
			add_ideas = STP_pc_val_compact
			diplomatic_relation = { country = VAL relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.21 hours = 1 }
		}
		add_ideas = STP_pc_val_compact
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pc_credit_val_compact = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pw_kefreyt_accounts_settled }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 900 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 900 }
		VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 900 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 900 } }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1800 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1800 }
		VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1800 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1800 } }
	}
	set_country_flag = STP_pw_kefreyt_accounts_settled
}

# Recipient accepts before either treasury changes; clear the receipt before delivery.
STP_pc_settle_nod_compact = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pc_nod_offer_current = yes STS = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_nod_offer_kind value = 1 compare = equals } STP_pc_can_pay_nod_compact = yes } }
		STS = {
			clear_variable = STP_pc_nod_offer_kind
			set_country_flag = STP_pc_nod_terms_accepted
			STP_pc_credit_nod_compact = yes

			diplomatic_relation = { country = NOD relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.21 hours = 1 }
		}
		add_ideas = STP_pc_nod_compact
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pc_credit_nod_compact = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1350 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1350 }
	NOD = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1350 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1350 } }
}

# COUNTRY STP: the programme kind owns the receipt until delivery or refund.
STP_pw_party_finish_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
			capital_scope = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 4 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 540 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 250 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 60 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 72 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_services value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 5 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 1080 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 1000 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 120 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 144 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_industry value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } STP_pw_party_has_port_capacity = yes }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 6 compare = equals } OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 900 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 250 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 100 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_deposit value = 120 compare = equals } } NOT = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_army value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_services value = 1 }
			add_stability = 0.05
			add_political_power = 75
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 5 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_industry value = 1 }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { naval_base > 0 free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = yes } }
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes }
				if = { limit = { infrastructure < 5 } add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pw_project_kind value = 6 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_army value = 1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1600 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 120 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 100 }
			army_experience = 25
		}
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_deposit
		clear_variable = STP_pw_project_kind
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_recovery_progress value = 1 }
		STP_pw_update_recovery = yes
	}
	else = { STP_pw_cancel_project = yes }
}

# COUNTRY NOD: payment and stock transfer are one accepted, rechecked settlement.
STP_pw_party_settle_nod_arms = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pw_party_nod_arms_current = yes }
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_arms_pending
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_arms_delivered
			subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 450 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 450 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 5 }
			STP_pf_shift = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 450 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 450 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = 1000 target = STP }
		send_equipment = { equipment = support_equipment amount = 100 target = STP }
		STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.24 hours = 1 } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY STS: the single receipt blocks other targets until delivery or refund.
STP_cw_refund_disruption = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = STP_cw_disruption_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_cash_refund value = STP_cw_disruption_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_cw_disruption_deposit
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_disruption_cooldown
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_cw_cash_refund } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_cw_cash_refund } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		add_political_power = 50
	}
}

# COUNTRY STS, FROM state: choose a currently present enemy brigade at settlement.
STP_cw_finish_disruption = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_disruption_deposit value = 100 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_cw_disruption_deposit value = 1200 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { STP_cw_disruption_available = yes FROM = { STP_cw_disruption_target = yes } }
			clear_variable = STP_cw_disruption_deposit
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_disruption_completed value = 1 }
			FROM = { random_state_division = { limit = { OWNER = { tag = STP } division_has_battalion_in_template = ADISCORD_territorial } destroy_unit = yes } }
		}
		else = { STP_cw_refund_disruption = yes }
	}
}

# Debug skips keep STP_cw_can_start intact: they write the election result, then
# call the production split. Settlement goes through .93 so ROOT is the loser.
STP_debug_ensure_union_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } STP = { exists = yes } }
		STP = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag } }
				set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			}
			STP_cw_open_preparation = yes
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_elections_finished
			STP_cw_start = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished } }
		STP = { STP_cw_begin_hostilities = yes }
	}
}

STP_debug_start_civil_war = {
	STP_debug_ensure_union_war = yes
}

STP_debug_resolve_party_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } }
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
			set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_party_election_victory
		}
	}
	STP_debug_ensure_union_war = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished } STP = { exists = yes } STS = { exists = yes } }
		if = { limit = { is_ai = no tag = STS } STP = { change_tag_from = STS } }
		STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.93 hours = 1 } }
	}
}

STP_debug_resolve_shabrat_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started } }
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_shabrat_election_victory
		}
	}
	STP_debug_ensure_union_war = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_started NOT = { has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished } STP = { exists = yes } STS = { exists = yes } }
		if = { limit = { is_ai = no tag = STP } STS = { change_tag_from = STP } }
		STP = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.93 hours = 1 } }
	}
}
