STP_pf_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pf_active = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty } } set_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 40 } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = 24 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = 16 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = 16 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = 18 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = 10 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = STP_apparatus_loyalty }
		set_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized
		STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	}
}

# Only mutations recalculate the seven contributions; GUI reads the cached values.
STP_pf_calculate = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution value = STP_pf_conservatives_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution value = STP_pf_conservatives_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_contribution value = STP_pf_borons_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_contribution value = STP_pf_borons_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_borons_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_contribution value = STP_pf_security_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_contribution value = STP_pf_security_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_security_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_contribution value = STP_pf_army_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_contribution value = STP_pf_army_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_army_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_contribution value = STP_pf_advisers_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_contribution value = STP_pf_advisers_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_advisers_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_contribution value = STP_pf_merchants_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_contribution value = STP_pf_merchants_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_merchants_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_frame value = 2 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_contribution value = STP_pf_radicals_support }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_contribution value = STP_pf_radicals_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_contribution value = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = STP_pf_radicals_contribution }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_frame value = 1 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_frame value = 2 } }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_effect value = STP_pf_conservatives_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_effect value = STP_pf_conservatives_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_effect value = -0.002 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_effect value = STP_pf_borons_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_effect value = STP_pf_borons_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_effect value = 0.002 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_effect value = STP_pf_security_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_effect value = STP_pf_security_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_effect value = 0.001 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_effect value = STP_pf_army_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_effect value = STP_pf_army_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_effect value = 0.002 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = STP_pf_advisers_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = STP_pf_advisers_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = 0.004 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_effect value = STP_pf_merchants_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_effect value = STP_pf_merchants_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_effect value = 0.003 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_effect value = STP_pf_radicals_support }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_effect value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_effect value = STP_pf_radicals_influence }
	divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_effect value = 100 }
	multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_effect value = -0.003 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_pf_nod_connected value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { STP_pf_nod_contacts = yes } set_variable = { var = STP_pf_nod_connected value = 1 } }
	else = { set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_effect value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { STP_pf_active = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pf_balance_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pf_balance_dynamic } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Input: selected faction 1..7. Retain explicit fields for the native loader.
STP_pf_shift = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_pf_active = yes has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 7 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 1 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_conservatives_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 2 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_borons_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 3 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_security_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 4 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_army_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 5 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_advisers_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 6 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_merchants_influence } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 7 compare = equals } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_old_influence value = STP_pf_radicals_influence } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_gain value = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_room value = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_room value = STP_pf_old_influence }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_room value = 5 compare = less_than } } set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_gain value = STP_pf_room } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_room value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_scale value = STP_pf_room }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_scale value = STP_pf_gain }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = STP_pf_room }
			multiply_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = STP_pf_scale }
			divide_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = STP_pf_room }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 4 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 5 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 6 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = 12 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 7 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = STP_pf_old_influence }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = STP_pf_gain }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = 12 }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = 12 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_conservatives_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_borons_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_security_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_army_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_advisers_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_merchants_influence }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_remainder value = STP_pf_radicals_influence }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 1 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 2 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 3 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 4 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 5 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 6 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 7 compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = STP_pf_remainder } }
		STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_pf_selected value = 0 }
}

STP_pf_clear = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized }
		clr_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized
		clr_country_flag = STP_pf_negotiation_cooldown
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pf_balance_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_pf_balance_dynamic } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_apparatus_loyalty_dynamic_modifier } }
		clear_variable = STP_pf_conservatives_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_conservatives_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_conservatives_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_conservatives_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_conservatives_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_borons_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_borons_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_borons_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_borons_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_borons_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_security_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_security_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_security_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_security_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_security_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_army_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_army_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_army_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_army_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_army_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_advisers_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_advisers_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_advisers_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_advisers_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_advisers_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_merchants_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_merchants_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_merchants_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_merchants_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_merchants_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_radicals_influence
		clear_variable = STP_pf_radicals_support
		clear_variable = STP_pf_radicals_contribution
		clear_variable = STP_pf_radicals_frame
		clear_variable = STP_pf_radicals_effect
		clear_variable = STP_pf_nod_connected
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# PARTY POSTWAR: refusing NOD's protectorate turns sovereignty into a timed invasion crisis.
STP_pw_party_start_nod_invasion_threat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_cw_postwar
			has_completed_focus = STP_pw_party_sovereignty
			is_subject = no
			NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NOD }
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		activate_mission = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_countdown
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.28 hours = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pw_party_launch_nod_invasion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
			is_subject = no
			has_capitulated = no
			NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no }
			NOT = { has_war_with = NOD }
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		NOD = {
			add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_pw_nod_invasion_mandate days = 365 }
			declare_war_on = { target = STP type = annex_everything }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.29 hours = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
}

STP_pw_party_settle_nod_invasion_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
			NOD = { exists = yes has_war_with = STP }
		}
		NOD = {
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = STP } white_peace = STP }
			remove_ideas = STP_pw_nod_invasion_mandate
			add_war_support = -0.10
		}
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
		clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_defeated
		add_stability = 0.08
		add_political_power = 100
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.30 hours = 1 }
	}
}

STP_pw_party_settle_nod_invasion_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = NOD
			STP = {
				exists = yes
				has_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
				has_war_with = NOD
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = STP_pw_nod_invasion_mandate
		set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_invasion_victorious
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = STP } white_peace = STP }
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_threat_active
			clr_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_active
			set_country_flag = STP_pw_party_nod_invasion_lost
		}
		annex_country = { target = STP transfer_troops = yes }
	}
}

# Party defensive recovery. Deposits survive a government relocation.
STP_ps_begin_defence = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_stage } }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 1 }
		STP_ps_refresh_defence = yes
		STP_ps_poll_nodes = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_refresh_defence = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_ps_breakthrough value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_ps_planning value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 1 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_breakthrough value = -0.45 } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_planning value = -0.5 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 2 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_breakthrough value = -0.3 } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_planning value = -0.3 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 3 compare = equals } } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_breakthrough value = -0.15 } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_planning value = -0.15 } }
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_defence_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_defence_dynamic } }
}
STP_ps_finish_stage = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes has_variable = STP_ps_reorg_deposit }
		# Read control at the actual deadline; the daily node pulse may run later.
		if = {
			limit = { STP_ps_government_operational = yes }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_reorg_deposit
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 1 }
			clamp_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage min = 1 max = 4 }
			STP_ps_refresh_defence = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days value = 1 }
			STP_ps_open_congress_crisis = yes
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_pause_reorganisation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_reorg_1 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_ps_reorg_1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days min = 1 max = 112 }
		remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_reorg_2 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_ps_reorg_2 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days min = 1 max = 112 }
		remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_reorg_3 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days value = days_mission_timeout@STP_ps_reorg_3 }
		clamp_variable = { var = STP_ps_paused_days min = 1 max = 112 }
		remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_3
	}
}
STP_ps_resume_reorganisation = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes STP_ps_government_operational = yes has_variable = STP_ps_paused_days }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_reorg_1
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = STP_ps_paused_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = 21 }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_reorg_1 days = STP_ps_days_delta }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_reorg_2
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = STP_ps_paused_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = 21 }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_reorg_2 days = STP_ps_days_delta }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_reorg_3
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = STP_ps_paused_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_days_delta value = 28 }
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_reorg_3 days = STP_ps_days_delta }
		}
		clear_variable = STP_ps_paused_days
	}
}
STP_ps_close_defence = {
	# Clear the receipt first: remove_mission can call cancel_effect recursively.
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_reorg_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_reorg_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_reorg_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	clear_variable = STP_ps_paused_days
	remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_1
	remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_2
	remove_mission = STP_ps_reorg_3
	remove_mission = STP_ps_congress_deadline
	clr_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_crisis
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_defence_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_defence_dynamic } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_nodes_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_nodes_dynamic } }
}
STP_ps_open_congress_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes STP_ps_holds_congress = no NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_crisis } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_crisis
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_fell } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_fell
			add_stability = -0.15
			add_war_support = -0.10
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change value = -20 }
			STP_change_apparatus_loyalty = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_government_operational = no }
			STP_ps_pause_reorganisation = yes
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions } } activate_mission = STP_ps_congress_deadline }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_resolve_congress_crisis = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_government_operational = yes }
		remove_mission = STP_ps_congress_deadline
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_holds_congress = yes } clr_country_flag = STP_ps_congress_crisis }
		STP_ps_resume_reorganisation = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_congress_timeout = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes STP_ps_government_operational = no NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions
		add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.10 }
		STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else = { STP_ps_resolve_congress_crisis = yes }
}
STP_ps_poll_nodes = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes }
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 145 NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_145 } } set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_145 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_145 NOT = { controls_province = 145 } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145
			STP_ps_open_congress_crisis = yes
			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 145 has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145
			STP_ps_resolve_congress_crisis = yes
			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16366 NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16366 } } set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16366 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16366 NOT = { controls_province = 16366 } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16366 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16366

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16366 has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16366 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16366

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 45 NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_45 } } set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_45 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_45 NOT = { controls_province = 45 } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_45 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_45

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 45 has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_45 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_45

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16345 NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16345 } } set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16345 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16345 NOT = { controls_province = 16345 } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16345 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16345

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16345 has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16345 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16345

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16445 NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16445 } } set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16445 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_seen_16445 NOT = { controls_province = 16445 } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16445 } }
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16445

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
		if = { limit = { controls_province = 16445 has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16445 }
			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_16445

			STP_ps_refresh_nodes = yes
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_refresh_nodes = {
	set_variable = { var = STP_ps_node_planning value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = STP_ps_emergency_expense value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_emergency_expense value = 0.10 } }
	set_variable = { var = STP_ps_node_redeployment value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_145 } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_node_planning value = -0.15 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_node_lost_45 } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_node_redeployment value = -0.20 } }
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_nodes_dynamic } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = STP_ps_nodes_dynamic } }
}

STP_ps_refund_cabinet = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_cabinet_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_cabinet_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_cabinet_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_finish_cabinet = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_war_active = yes STP_ps_government_operational = no has_variable = STP_ps_cabinet_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_cabinet_deposit
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_temporary_government
		STP_ps_resolve_congress_crisis = yes
	}
	else = { STP_ps_refund_cabinet = yes }
}

			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}

			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 480 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 240 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = 160 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_ammunition_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = {
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money
	clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_route_wait
	remove_mission = STP_ps_nod_delivery
}
STP_ps_refund_nod = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type }
		STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = yes
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1200 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_dispatch_nod = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_order_current = yes STP_ps_nod_supply_route = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 2 }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_delivery
		}
		else_if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_order_current = yes } activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_route_wait }
		else = { STP_ps_refund_nod = yes }
	}
}
STP_ps_deliver_nod = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type }
		STP_ps_clear_nod_receipt = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 1 compare = equals } } set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_fund_nod_cooldown value = 1 days = 42 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } } set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_arm_nod_cooldown value = 1 days = 28 } }
		if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 1 compare = equals } }
				NOD = {
					ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1500 }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1500 }
					set_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit
					ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
				NOD = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1200 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 3 compare = equals } NOD = { STP_ps_northern_front_active = yes } }
				NOD = { add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_engineers_idea days = 60 } }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_receipt_type value = 4 compare = equals } NOD = { OR = { NOD_cw_intervention_possible = yes AND = { has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered has_war_with = STS } } } }
				NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready STP_ps_apply_expedition = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_apply_expedition = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready has_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered has_war_with = STS }
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_ready
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_expedition_used
		add_timed_idea = { idea = STP_ps_expedition_idea days = 42 }
	}
}

STP_ps_request_northern_agreement = {
	if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_agreement_requested } } }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_agreement_requested country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.200 } }
	}
}

STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_northern_front_active = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_deposit


			clr_country_flag = STP_ps_nod_procurement_credit add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2000 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_nod_rifle_purchase_refund = yes }
	}
}

# Accepted STP defeat only. Settle outgoing resources before annexation.
STP_ps_prepare_exile = {
	if = { limit = { tag = STP NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_settled } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_settled
		STP_ps_close_defence = yes
		STP_ps_refund_cabinet = yes
		STP_ps_build_radio_refund = yes
		STP_ps_build_hq_refund = yes
		STP_ps_prepare_evacuation_refund = yes
		STP_ps_transport_refund = yes
		STP_ps_ammunition_refund = yes
		STP_ps_secure_routes_refund = yes
		STP_ps_reserve_training_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_intercept_refund = yes
		STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = yes

		STP_ps_refund_nod = yes
		# An outgoing convoy is settled once before the donor ceases to exist.
		STP_ps_deliver_nod = yes
		if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes OR = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_agreement AND = { is_ai = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } } } }
			STP_ps_settle_exile = yes
		}
		else_if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes is_ai = no NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } }
			STP_ps_snapshot_exile = yes
			NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_pending country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.201 } }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_snapshot_exile = {
	NOD = {
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_money value = STP.STP_ps_evacuated_money }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_loyalty value = STP.STP_apparatus_loyalty }
	}
	clear_variable = STP_ps_evacuated_money

	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_conservatives_support value = STP.STP_pf_conservatives_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_conservatives_influence value = STP.STP_pf_conservatives_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_borons_support value = STP.STP_pf_borons_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_borons_influence value = STP.STP_pf_borons_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_security_support value = STP.STP_pf_security_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_security_influence value = STP.STP_pf_security_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_army_support value = STP.STP_pf_army_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_army_influence value = STP.STP_pf_army_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_advisers_support value = STP.STP_pf_advisers_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_advisers_influence value = STP.STP_pf_advisers_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_merchants_support value = STP.STP_pf_merchants_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_merchants_influence value = STP.STP_pf_merchants_influence } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_radicals_support value = STP.STP_pf_radicals_support } }
	NOD = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_exile_radicals_influence value = STP.STP_pf_radicals_influence } }
}
STP_ps_settle_exile = {
	STP_ps_snapshot_exile = yes
	NOD = { STP_ps_accept_exile = yes }
}
STP_ps_accept_exile = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received } }
		set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_pending
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_credit value = STP_ps_exile_money }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_money
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_credit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_credit }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		# Only an existing character can move; no recruitment of dead officeholders.
		STP = { if = { limit = { STP_ps_hedersett_available = yes }
				STP_rufus_hedersett = { remove_country_leader_role = { ideology = aristocratic_hedonism } }
				set_nationality = { character = STP_rufus_hedersett target_country = NOD }
			} }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.202 }
	}
}


STP_ps_begin_return = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_can_launch_return = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = STS } } declare_war_on = { target = STS type = annex_everything } }
		# A rejected declaration must leave the decision available for another attempt.
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = STS } set_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign }
	}
}
STP_ps_settle_return = {
	# Actual NOD victory, never a paid project's completion.
	if = { limit = { tag = NOD has_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign STP = { exists = no } STS = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		STP_ps_close_exile = yes
		STS = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD } white_peace = NOD }
			every_owned_state = { limit = { is_core_of = STP } STP = { transfer_state = PREV } }
		}
		STP = {
			if = { limit = { exists = yes }
				set_country_flag = STP_ps_restored_dependency
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_conservatives_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_conservatives_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_conservatives_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_borons_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_borons_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_borons_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_security_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_security_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_security_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_army_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_army_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_army_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_advisers_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_advisers_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_advisers_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_merchants_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_merchants_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_merchants_influence }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_support value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_radicals_support }
				set_variable = { var = STP_pf_radicals_influence value = NOD.STP_ps_exile_radicals_influence }
				set_country_flag = STP_pf_initialized
				STP_refresh_apparatus_loyalty = yes
				set_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
				clr_country_flag = STP_sided_with_Maksim_flag
				STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes
				every_owned_state = { set_state_controller_to = STP }
				load_focus_tree = { tree = STP_cw_focus keep_completed = yes }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { STP = { exists = yes } }
			set_autonomy = { target = STP autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet }
			if = { limit = { STP_ps_hedersett_available = yes }
				set_nationality = { character = STP_rufus_hedersett target_country = STP }
				STP = { STP_rufus_hedersett = { add_country_leader_role = { country_leader = { ideology = aristocratic_hedonism expire = "2300.1.1" } } } promote_character = STP_rufus_hedersett }
			}
			else = { STP = { create_country_leader = { name = STP_ps_return_council_name desc = STP_ps_return_council_desc ideology = aristocratic_hedonism expire = "2300.1.1" } } }
			if = { limit = { is_ai = no } country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.203 } }
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_exile_training_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_exile_training_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_training_deposit
			set_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_training_completed

			army_experience = 15
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_exile_training_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_institution_payment_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_institution_payment_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = STP STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes OR = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_permanent_security has_completed_focus = STP_ps_district_fund } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_institution_stage value = 3 compare = less_than } }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_institution_payment_deposit


			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_institution_stage value = 1 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_institution_payment_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_finalize_centre = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_stability_factor value = 0.05 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_political_power_gain value = 0.10 }
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.08 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.10 }
		clr_country_flag = STP_ps_district_concessions
	}
	STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}
STP_ps_finalize_districts = {
	add_to_variable = { var = STP_pw_production_speed_buildings_factor value = 0.08 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_political_power_gain value = 0.05 }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = STP_pw_ADISCORD_economy_admin_expense_factor value = 0.05 }
	STP_pw_refresh_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

STP_ps_begin_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_supply_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_authorized NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles } NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_supply_cooldown } NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 1200 } } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 1200 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_partial_refund producer = VAL }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid }
			clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles value = 1200 }
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_sequence value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 1 }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_first_dispatch } }
				set_country_flag = STP_ps_val_first_dispatch
				add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_val_departure days = 7 }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_active } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_val_departure days = 14 } }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = {
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_money
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_known_sequence
	clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intercepted_sequence
	set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_supply_cooldown value = 1 days = 7 }
	remove_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
	remove_mission = STP_ps_val_route_wait
}
STP_ps_refund_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund_rifles value = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund_money value = STP_ps_val_receipt_money }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract }
		STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_refund_rifles producer = VAL }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 96 producer = VAL }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = VAL }
			add_manpower = 12600
		}
		if = { limit = { STS = { exists = yes } }
			STS = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = PREV.STP_ps_refund_money } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = PREV.STP_ps_refund_money } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		}
	}
}
STP_ps_dispatch_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_supply_open = no } STP_ps_refund_val_supply = yes }
		else_if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_land_route = yes }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 2 }
			STP_ps_resolve_val_supply = yes
		}
		else = { activate_mission = STP_ps_val_route_wait }
	}
}
STP_ps_resolve_val_supply = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_supply_open = no } STP_ps_refund_val_supply = yes }
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_rifles value = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_intercepted_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence compare = equals } STP = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 1 }
			}
			STP_ps_clear_val_receipt = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_cargo_intercepted value = 1 compare = equals } }
				STP = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles producer = VAL } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } }
					STP = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = 96 producer = VAL } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = VAL } }
				}
			}
			else_if = { limit = { STS = { exists = yes has_war_with = STP } }
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = less_than } }
					STP_ps_deliver_val_contract = yes
				}
				else = { STS = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles producer = VAL } } }
			}
			else = {
				# Existing state cache follows the actual split and inspection accounting.
				1 = { add_to_variable = { var = STP_cw_cached_rifles value = PREV.STP_ps_cargo_rifles } }
				STP = { STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } } ADISCORD_economy_receive_100 = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 1 compare = equals } } ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 3 compare = equals } }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VAL_export_income_1 } } add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_export_income_1 days = 70 } }
				else = { add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_export_income_2 days = 70 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_val_intelligence_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit }
		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_intelligence_available = yes check_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_intelligence_sequence value = VAL.STP_ps_val_sequence compare = equals } OR = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply }  }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intelligence_deposit


			VAL = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_known_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence } }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_val_intelligence_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_val_intercept_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_intercept_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_val_intercept_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intercept_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }

		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes

		if = { limit = { STP_ps_val_intelligence_available = yes check_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_intercept_sequence value = VAL.STP_ps_val_sequence compare = equals } OR = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply } VAL = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_known_sequence value = STP_ps_val_sequence compare = equals } STP_ps_val_land_route = yes } }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_val_intercept_deposit

STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_val_pressure_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = STP OR = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes STP_ps_war_active = yes } OR = { has_completed_focus = STP_ps_foreign_supply_desk has_completed_focus = STP_ps_counter_supply } VAL = { exists = yes } }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_val_pressure_deposit

			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_cooldown value = 1 days = 60 }
			VAL = { set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_val_pressure_active value = 1 days = 60 } }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_val_pressure_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_deliver_val_contract = {
	STS = {
		# Deliver free reserves here; the full package also pays for the units below.
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 3200 producer = VAL }
		if = {
			limit = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_contract_type value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			division_template = {
				name = "Kefreyt Contract Infantry"
				template_counter = 3
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				override_model = ADISCORD_VAL_regular_entity
				division_names_group = VAL_INF_01
				regiments = {
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
					infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
				}
				support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Kefreyt Contract Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.5 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = PREV
					count = 2
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_schedule_opposition_second = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = STP
			has_country_flag = STP_ps_first_opposition_finished
			has_active_mission = STP_cw_election_window
			check_variable = { var = days_mission_timeout@STP_cw_election_window value = 56 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			STP_cw_preparation_open = yes
			has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active
			has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
			NOT = { has_active_mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second }
		}
		2 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		3 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		29 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		46 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		53 = { clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second }
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = { state = 2 state = 3 state = 29 state = 46 state = 53 }
				STP_region_is_operable = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_cw_opposition_target }
				is_controlled_by = STP
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
					NOT = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
					AND = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					AND = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } }
					AND = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } }
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
		}
		if = { limit = { any_owned_state = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second } } activate_mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced } }
			set_country_flag = STP_cw_first_opposition_announced
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = 32 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = STP_apparatus_loyalty
				check_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty value = 25 compare = less_than }
			}
			add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_opposition_preparation_second days = -7 }
		}
	}
}

STP_cw_prepare_opposition_second = {
	if = {
		limit = { STP_cw_preparation_open = yes has_country_flag = STP_battle_for_stelander_active has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second STP_region_is_operable = yes is_controlled_by = STP }
			clr_state_flag = STP_ps_opposition_target_second
			# Administration must have existed before this operation to prepare arms.
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
				if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 2 state = 29 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset } }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_garrison_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { OR = { state = 3 state = 46 } NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset } STP = { STP_cw_can_spend_1200 = yes } }
					STP = { STP_cw_spend_1200 = yes }
					set_state_flag = STP_resistance_supply_asset
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 53 NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset } STP = { NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 240 } } } }
					STP = {
						set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 240 }
						STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
						set_variable = { var = STP_cw_sabotage_rifles value = 240 }
						set_state_flag = STP_resistance_sabotage_asset
					}
				}
			}
			STP = { add_power_balance_value = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy value = 0.04 } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_region_influence_change value = 12 }
			STP_add_resistance_influence = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = STP_cw_loyalty_minus_6_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				STP = {
					set_temp_variable = { var = STP_apparatus_loyalty_change value = -6 }
					STP_change_apparatus_loyalty = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { STP_region_resistance_entrenched = yes }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset } } set_state_flag = STP_resistance_administration_asset }
			}
			log = "[STP preparation] Announced district operation completed in [THIS.GetName]"
		}
		# The election deadline alone triggers the split. A disrupted operation
		# still spends its announced time before the next target is selected.
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_preparation.5 days = 1 } }
		STP_cw_refresh_army_report = yes
	}
}

# STATE: only the state that holds the receipt can release the shared deposit.
STP_ps_refund_district = {
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = STP_ps_district_paid }
		clr_state_flag = STP_ps_district_paid
		STP = { if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_district_deposit }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_district_deposit }
				clear_variable = STP_ps_district_deposit
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			} }
	}
}

STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_refund value = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit
		add_manpower = 6000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 600 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = STP_ps_refund }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = STP_ps_refund }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}
STP_ps_exile_formation_finish = {
	if = { limit = { has_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit }
		if = { limit = { tag = NOD STP_ps_exile_formation_context = yes has_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received }
			clear_variable = STP_ps_exile_formation_deposit

			set_country_flag = { flag = STP_ps_exile_formation_cooldown value = 1 days = 28 }
			STP_ps_deliver_exile_formation = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = yes }
	}
}

STP_ps_deliver_exile_formation = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Party Exile Brigade" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Party Exile Brigade"
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = no
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_territorial = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	random_owned_controlled_state = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Party Exile Brigade\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

STP_ps_pay_nod_arms = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 1200 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_rifle_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
	STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 60 }
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { PREV = { OR = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } } }
		PREV = {
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = STP_ps_artillery_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_ps_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = STP_ps_before }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = STP_ps_support_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_ps_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = STP_ps_before }
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid check_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
		clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 300 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_type value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_money value = 300 }
		set_variable = { var = STP_ps_nod_receipt_stage value = 1 }
		activate_mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch
		add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = STP_ps_nod_dispatch days = 13 }
	}
	else = {
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_rifle_before value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_artillery_before value = num_equipment@artillery_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_support_before value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_rifle_before }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = STP_ps_artillery_before }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = STP_ps_support_before }
	}
}

STP_ps_authorize_val_supply = {
	set_country_flag = STP_ps_val_authorized
}

STP_ps_request_asylum = {
	if = { limit = { NOD = { STP_ps_nod_can_host_exiles = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested } } }
		NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_ps_asylum_requested country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.204 } }
	}
}

# STS buyer; the existing acceptance gate has checked both parties and the price.
STP_ps_reserve_export_contract = {
	if = { limit = { VAL = { NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles } } }
		VAL = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_cw_rifle_cost value = 2500 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
			STP_cw_pay_rifles = yes
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_partial_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = STP_ps_partial_refund producer = VAL }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { VAL = { has_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_spend_100 = yes
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
			VAL = {
				clr_country_flag = STP_cw_rifles_paid
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_money value = PREV.STP_ps_before }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles value = 2500 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract value = 3 }
				set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 1 }
				add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_sequence value = 1 }
				activate_mission = STP_ps_val_departure
			}
		}
	}
}

# NOD: settle fixed-price exile orders before closing or restoring the party.
STP_ps_close_exile = {
	STP_ps_exile_formation_refund = yes
	STP_ps_exile_training_refund = yes
	remove_mission = STP_ps_exile_formation_work
	remove_mission = STP_ps_exile_training_work
	clr_country_flag = STP_ps_exile_received
	clr_country_flag = STP_ps_return_campaign
	set_country_flag = STP_ps_return_closed
	if = { limit = { has_template = "Party Exile Brigade" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Party Exile Brigade" is_locked = no }
	}
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
}

# --- occidia_reintegration ---
STP_occidia_start_campaign = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing STP_occidia_invasion_available = yes }
        set_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
        clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing
        SRP = { remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
        declare_war_on = { target = SRP type = annex_everything }
    }
    else = { STP_occidia_cancel_preparation = yes }
}

STP_occidia_cancel_preparation = {
    clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_preparing
    SRP = { remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
}

STP_occidia_settle_victory = {
    if = {
        limit = { STP_occidia_victory_ready = yes }
        clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
        SRP = { STP_cw_finish_mobilization = yes remove_mission = STP_occidia_invasion_warning }
        annex_country = { target = SRP transfer_troops = no }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
}

# A peace imposed elsewhere or the attacker's defeat closes this campaign too.
STP_occidia_reconcile_peace = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active NOT = { has_war_with = SRP } }
        clr_country_flag = STP_occidia_campaign_active
    }
}
